1b1(sher phyin): @#//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag nyi shu lnga pa/dum bu gsum pa bam po bzhi bcu 
1b2(sher phyin): gcig pa/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di ni 'dus byas kyi khams 'dor ba ma yin/'dus ma byas kyi khams 
1b3(sher phyin): sgrub pa ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams byung yang rung /de bzhin gshegs pa rnam ma 
1b4(sher phyin): byung yang rung /chos rnams kyi chos nyid dang chos kyi dbyings dang /chos kyi gnas nyid dang /chos skyon pa med pa de ni de 
1b5(sher phyin): bzhin du gnas pa yin te/de la ni de bzhin gshegs pas mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas so//mngon par thugs su 
1b6(sher phyin): chung ngo //mngon par rdzogs par sangasargyas te/mngon par rdzogs par thugs su chud nas kyang gsung ngo //ston to//rjod 
1b7(sher phyin): do/'grel to/rnam par 'byed do/gsal bar mdzad do/yang dag par ston to//de nas lha'i bu stong phrag mang po steng 
1b8(sher phyin): gi bar snang la 'khor nas lhung lhung ldegs kyi sgra phyung ste/lha rdzas kyi me tog ud pa la dang /pad mo dang /ku mud dang /pun da 
2a1(sher phyin): @#//
2b1(sher phyin): med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams dang /rnam par thar pa brgyad dang /mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug par dgu dang /stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa dang /mngon par shes pa rnams mngon par 
2b2(sher phyin): shes pa rnams kyis stong ngo //ting nge 'dzin rnams dang /gzungs kyi sgo rnams gzungs kyi sgo rnams kyis stong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams dang /mi 'jigs pa rnams dang /so so yang dag par rig pa rnams dang /
2b3(sher phyin): byams pa chen po dang snying rje chen po dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dre pa rnams kyis stong ngo //rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bus stong ngo //lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 
2b4(sher phyin): 'bras bu dang /phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bus stong ngo /dgra bcom pa nyid dgra bcom pa nyid kyis stong ngo //rang byang chub rang byang chub kyis stong ngo //lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid
2b5(sher phyin): kyis stong ngo /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i nyid kyis stong ngo //gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni chen mo ste/
2b6(sher phyin): 'di ltar shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa rang bzhin gyis stong pa'o//de ni chos thams cad kyang chos thams cad kyis stong la/byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po rnams kyang /shes rab kyi pha rol du phyinapa la 
2b7(sher phyin): gnas shing /bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya yang /chos gang yang mngon par rdzogs par 'tshad rgya bar ma mchis/chos kyi 'khor lo yang skor la chos gang yang 'khor ba 
2b8(sher phyin): 'am mi 'khor ba'am rab tu 'khor bar bgyid pa ma mchis te/de dag gis chos gang yang yongs su mthong ba'am yongs su ma mthong ba ma mchis so//de ci'i slad du zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang 'khor bar bgyi ba'am mi 'khor
3a1(sher phyin): @#//bar bgyi ba 'am rab tu 'khor bar bgyi ba'i chos de lta bu dmigs su ma mchis te/de ni 'di ltar chos thams cad kyang shin tu mngon par ma grub pa'o//de ci'i slad tu zhe na/stong pa nyid ni 'khor bar bgyid pa 'am mi 
3a2(sher phyin): 'khor bar bgyid pa ma lags so//mtshan ma ma mchis pa ni 'khor bar bgyid pa'am mi 'khor bar bgyid pa ma lag so//smon pa ma mchis pa ni 'khor ba bgyid pa 'am mi 'khor bar bgyid ba ma lags so//de ltar na 
3a3(sher phyin): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bstan pa dang /rab tu bstan pa dang bsgrags pa dang rab tu dgod pa dang gzhag pa dang rnam par dbye ba dang rnam par dgrol ba dang smos pa dang gsal bar bgyi ba dang yang dag par bstan pa 'di 
3a4(sher phyin): ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa bstan pa yongs su dag pa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa bstan pa de yang sus kyang ma bstan sus kyang ma bzung ngo//gang sus kyang ma bstan sus kyi ma bzung ba de ni sus kyang mngon du ma 
3a5(sher phyin): bgyis so//gang sus kyang mngon du ma bgyis pa de la ni yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba ma mchis te/chos bstan pa 'di la gang su yang sbyin pa'i gnas su 'gyur ba ma mchis so////
3a6(sher phyin): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa stong phrag nyi shu lnga pa las le'u nyi shu brgyad pa'o////de nas tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste/shes rab kyi pha 
3a7(sher phyin): rol tu phyin pa 'di ni ma mchis pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/nam mkha' med pa'i phyir ro//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni 
3a8(sher phyin): mnyam pa nyid kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//btsom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa/chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi phyir ro//gsol pa/btsom ldan 'das 'di lta ste//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di ni rnam par 
3b1(sher phyin): dben pa'i pha rol du phyin pa'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid kyi phyir ro//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di ni thub pa med pa'i pha rol
3b2(sher phyin): du phyin pa'o||bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'-i stsal pa/chos thams cad dmigs su med pa'i phyir ro//gsol pa/bco ma ldan 'das 'di lta ste//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyinapa 'di ni gnas ma mchis pa'i pha rol du phyin
3b3(sher phyin): pa'o||bcom ldan 'das ky-is bka' stsal pa/ming dang lus med pa'i phyir ro//gsolapa/bcom ldan 'das 'di la ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni nam mkha'i pha rol du phyin pa'o/bcom ldan 'das
3b4(sher phyin): kyis bka' stsal pa/dbugs phyi nang du rgyu ba med pa'i phyir ro//gsolas pa/bcom ldan 'das 'd-i lta ste/shes rab kyi pha ril tu phyin pa 'di ni brjod tu ma mchis pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis
3b5(sher phyin): bka'-i stsal pa/rnam par rtog pa dang/rnam par dpyod pa dmigs su med pa'i phyir//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di ni ming ma mchis pa'i pha rol du phy-in pa'o/
3b6(sher phyin): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/tshor ba dang/'du shes dang 'du byed dang rnam par shes pa dmigs su med pa'i phy-ir ro//gsol pa/bcom ldana'adas 'di lta ste/shes rab kyi pha rol du phy-in pa
3b7(sher phyin): 'di ni 'gro ba ma mchis pa'i pha rol du phyin pa'o//bcom ldana'adas kyis bka' stsal pa/chos thams cad 'gro ba med pa'i phyir ro//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'da'as 'di lta ste/shes rab ky-i pha rol du phyin pa 'di n-i mi 'phrogs pa'i pha rol du
3b8(sher phyin): phyin pa'o//bcom ldan 'angs kyis bka' stsal pa/chosathams cad gzung du med pa'i phyir ro//gsol pa/bcom dan 'das 'di lta ste/shes rabakyi pha rol du phyin pa 'd-i ni skad cig pa ma mchis pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//bcom

4b4(sher phyin): mongs pa dmigs su med pa'i phying ro//gsol pa/bcom lngan 'das 'di lta ste/shesarabakyi pha rol du phyin pa 'i ni rnam par byang ba ma mchis pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//bcom ldana'adas kyis bka' stsal pa/nyon mongs pa med pa'i phyir ro//

9b1(sher phyin): dpa' chen po dang 'dra bar gzung bar bgyi'o//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di ni zab pa ste/sngon pha rol du phyin pa drug la ma spyad pas ni/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin
9b2(sher phyin): zab mo 'di thos nas kyang mos par bgyi bar mi nus so//bcom ldan 'das rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyid pa zab mo 'di thos nas spang bar sems pa dag gis ni sngon gyi
9b3(sher phyin): mthar yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di spangs bar gyur to//de ci'i slad du zhe na /de ni 'di ltar rigs kyi bu 'am rigs gyi bu mo de dag shes rab kyi pha rol du pyin pa zab mo 'di bshad pa'i tshe/
9b4(sher phyin): dad pa dang mos pa ma mchis pa'i slad du ste/rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi ba mo de dag gis sngon gyi mthar yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dang/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 
9b5(sher phyin): rnams dang/nyan thos rnams la bsnyen bkur ma bgyis te/sbyin pa'i pha rol du phyin pa la ni ji ltar spyad par bgyi/tshul khrims kyi pha rol du phyin pa dang/bzod pa'i pha rol du phyin pa dang /brtson 
9b6(sher phyin): 'grus kyi pha rol du phyin pa dad/bsam gtan gyi pha rol du phyin pa dang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa la niji ltar spyad par bgyi/nang stong pa nyid ni ji ltar bsgom par bgyi/dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid 
9b7(sher phyin): kyi bar ni ji ltar bsgom par bgya/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams ni ji ltar bsgom par bagyi/yang dag par spong ba rnams dang/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams dang/dbang po rnams dang/stobs rnams dang/byang
9b8(sher phyin): chub kyi yan lag rnams dang/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa ni ji ltar bsgom par ba gyi/'phags pa'i bden pa rnams dang /bsam gtan rnams dang/tshad med pa rnams dang/gzugs med pa'i 
10a1(sher phyin): snyoms par 'jug pa rnams dang /rnam par thar pa brgyad dang /mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu dang /stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa dang /mngon par 
10a2(sher phyin): shes pa rnams ni ji ltar bsgom par bgyi/ting nge 'dzin rnams dang /gzungs kyi sgo rnams ni ji ltar bsgom pa bgyi/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams dang /mi 'jigs pa rnams dang /so so 
10a3(sher phyin): yang dag par rig pa rnams dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bcwa brgyad ni ji ltar bsgom par bgyi zhes yongs su ma zhus/yongs su ma dris so//de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis/tshe dang ldan pa 
10a4(sher phyin): shA ra dwa ta'i bu la 'di skad ces smras so//btsun pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di ni shin du zab na/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyinapa zab mo 'di bstan pa'i tshe/sngon ma spyad pa'i byang chub 
10a5(sher phyin): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po mos par mi 'gyur ba lta ngo mtshar kyang ci che/sbyin pa'i pha rol du phyin pa dang /tshul khrims kyi pha rol du phyin pa dang bzod pa'i pha rol du phyin pa dag/brtson 'grus 
10a6(sher phyin): kyi pha rol du phyin pa dag bsam gtan gyi pha rol du phyin pa dag/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dag nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi bar 
10a7(sher phyin): dang /byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos sum bcu rtsa bdun dang /'phags pa'i bden pa rnams dang /bsam gtan rnams dang tshad med pa rnams dang gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams dang /rnam par thar pa
